<blockquote>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="path-parameters"></div><div class="heading-text">Path Parameters</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Path Parameters" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#path-parameters"></a></h2>
</blockquote>

















<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>version</td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>The requested API version. Defaults to <code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">1</code>.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<blockquote>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="headers"></div><div class="heading-text">Headers</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Headers" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#headers"></a></h2>
</blockquote>























<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>X-Tenant</td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Tenant ID required to access this API.</td></tr><tr><td>X-Api-Key</td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>API key required for authentication. To see how to get it, check this <a href="https://docs.zrexpress.app/docs/authentication#/" target="" title="">guide</a>.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<blockquote>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="body-parameters"></div><div class="heading-text">Body Parameters</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Body Parameters" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#body-parameters"></a></h2>
</blockquote>













































































<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">Name</th><th style="text-align:left">Type</th><th style="text-align:left">Required</th><th style="text-align:left">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">name</td><td style="text-align:left">string</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Product’s name. It must be between 2 and 200 characters and cannot contain the pipe character&quot; | &quot;.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">categoryId</td><td style="text-align:left">uuid</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Unique identifier of the main product category.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">subCategoryId</td><td style="text-align:left">uuid</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Unique identifier of the product’s subcategory.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">basePrice</td><td style="text-align:left">double</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Selling price of the product. It must be greater than 0</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">purchasePrice</td><td style="text-align:left">double</td><td style="text-align:left">No</td><td style="text-align:left">Price paid by the company to acquire the product. If provided, it must be greater than 0.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">length</td><td style="text-align:left">double</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Length of the product (in cm).</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">width</td><td style="text-align:left">double</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Width of the product (in cm).</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">height</td><td style="text-align:left">double</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Height of the product (in cm).</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">weight</td><td style="text-align:left">double</td><td style="text-align:left">No</td><td style="text-align:left">Weight of the product (in kg).</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">localStock</td><td style="text-align:left">int32</td><td style="text-align:left">Yes</td><td style="text-align:left">Quantity of the product available in local stock.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left">sku</td><td style="text-align:left">string</td><td style="text-align:left">No</td><td style="text-align:left">Stock Keeping Unit — a unique internal code used to track the product in inventory.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<blockquote>
<p><strong>Remark:</strong> Use the API endpoint<br/>
<code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">https://api.zrexpress.app/api/v{version}/categories/search</code><br/>
to get the list of categories with their subcategories.</p>
</blockquote>
<br/>
<blockquote>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="request-example"></div><div class="heading-text">Request Example</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Request Example" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#request-example"></a></h2>
</blockquote>
<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="curl">cURL</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">curl --request POST \
     --url https://api.zrexpress.app/api/v1/products \
     --header &#x27;accept: application/json&#x27; \
     --header &#x27;X-Tenant: YOUR-TENANT-ID&#x27; \
     --header &#x27;content-type: application/json&#x27; \
     --header  &#x27;X-Api-Key: YOUR-API-KEY&#x27;
     --data &#x27;{
       &quot;name&quot;: &quot;Chocolat Lindt au lait&quot;,
       &quot;sku&quot;: &quot;LINDT-CHOC-100G-MLK&quot;,
       &quot;basePrice&quot;: 1250,
       &quot;purchasePrice&quot;: 850,
       &quot;length&quot;: 22,
       &quot;width&quot;: 12,
       &quot;height&quot;: 1,
       &quot;weight&quot;: 0.2,
       &quot;localStock&quot;: 20,
       &quot;categoryId&quot;: &quot;2e2edec1-1be7-46c9-b403-ae66ee4f1b6f&quot;,
       &quot;subCategoryId&quot;: &quot;944b343c-9f7b-4e36-9921-4dc2c90a2add&quot;
     }&#x27;</code></pre></div></div>
<blockquote>
<h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="response"></div><div class="heading-text">Response</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Response" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#response"></a></h2>
<p>If the product is successfully created, you will receive a <code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">201 Created</code> response containing the product ID.</p>
</blockquote>
<p><strong>Status<code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">201 Created</code> Response Body</strong></p>















<div class="rdmd-table"><div class="rdmd-table-inner"><table><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>id</td><td>uuid</td><td>The unique identifier of the created resource.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<hr/>
<blockquote>
<p>If validation of any input fails, the API will return a <code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">400 Bad Request</code> error with details about the failed validations.</p>
</blockquote>
<p><strong>Status<code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">400 Bad Request</code> Response Body Example</strong></p>
<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;type&quot;: &quot;https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc7231#section-6.5.1&quot;,
  &quot;title&quot;: &quot;General.Validation&quot;,
  &quot;status&quot;: 400,
  &quot;detail&quot;: &quot;One or more validation errors occurred&quot;,
  &quot;errors&quot;: [
    {
      &quot;code&quot;: &quot;GreaterThanValidator&quot;,
      &quot;description&quot;: &quot;Base price must be greater than 0.&quot;,
      &quot;type&quot;: 2
    },
    {
      &quot;code&quot;: &quot;GreaterThanValidator&quot;,
      &quot;description&quot;: &quot;Purchase price must be greater than 0.&quot;,
      &quot;type&quot;: 2
    }
  ],
  &quot;traceId&quot;: &quot;00-a0ffff7dcd26883866e64624673922a5-4247823a32b02a58-01&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div>
<br/>
<blockquote>
<p>If any requested resource (e.g., category or subcategory) does not exist, the API will return a 404 Not Found error.</p>
</blockquote>
<p><strong>Status<code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">404 Not Found</code> Response Body Example</strong></p>
<div class="CodeTabs CodeTabs_initial theme-undefined"><div class="CodeTabs-toolbar"><button type="button" value="json">JSON</button></div><div class="CodeTabs-inner"><pre><code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">{
  &quot;type&quot;: &quot;https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc7231#section-6.5.4&quot;,
  &quot;title&quot;: &quot;Categories.NotFound&quot;,
  &quot;status&quot;: 404,
  &quot;detail&quot;: &quot;The category with the identifier 2e2edec1-1be7-46c9-b403-ae66ee4f1b11 was not found&quot;,
  &quot;traceId&quot;: &quot;00-f93e5d4c017ac576574389cedc66987b-215e10275393014f-01&quot;
}</code></pre></div></div></div><form class="param-type rm-APISchema" name="Parameters"><header id="path-params" data-testid="APISchema-SectionHeader" align="center" class="Flex Flex_row
